Full unmodified RDF Turtle:
```turtle
@prefix ex: <https://w3id.org/nltl/benchmark/traficom-batch-i/TRF-120-P01/> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

ex:thrusterBody a nltl:thrusterBody ;
    nltl:hasNaturalFrequencyCalculationCase ex:longitudinalCase,
        ex:transverseCase .

ex:longitudinalCase a nltl:calculationCase ;
    nltl:shipAttachmentStiffness ex:shipAttachmentStiffnessValue ;
    nltl:thrusterNaturalFrequency ex:thrusterNaturalFrequencyValue ;
    nltl:vibrationDirection nltl:longitudinalDirection ;
    nltl:waterAddedMass ex:waterAddedMassValue .

ex:transverseCase a nltl:calculationCase ;
    nltl:shipAttachmentStiffness ex:shipAttachmentStiffnessValue ;
    nltl:thrusterNaturalFrequency ex:thrusterNaturalFrequencyValue ;
    nltl:vibrationDirection nltl:transverseDirection ;
    nltl:waterAddedMass ex:waterAddedMassValue .
```

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-120-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:damping ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Damping must be recorded for the thruster global natural-frequency estimation."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:waterAddedMass ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:KiloGM ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Water added mass, expressed in kilograms, must be provided."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:thrusterNaturalFrequency ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:HZ ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Thruster natural frequency, expressed in hertz, must be provided."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasComponent ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class nltl:thrusterBody ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:shipAttachmentStiffness ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:KiloN-PER-M ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ] ;
                sh:message "Ship attachment stiffness, expressed in kilonewtons per metre, must be provided for the thruster body."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:hasNaturalFrequencyCalculationCase ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class nltl:calculationCase ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:vibrationDirection ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:or (
                            [
                                sh:hasValue nltl:longitudinalDirection
                            ]
                            [
                                sh:hasValue nltl:transverseDirection
                            ]
                        ) ;
                        sh:message "Each natural-frequency calculation case must identify a longitudinal or transverse vibration direction."
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ] .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-120-CalculationCaseShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:calculationCase ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:vibrationDirection ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:or (
            [
                sh:hasValue nltl:longitudinalDirection
            ]
            [
                sh:hasValue nltl:transverseDirection
            ]
        ) ;
        sh:message "Each natural-frequency calculation case must identify a longitudinal or transverse vibration direction."
    ] .
```

The ultimate load resulting from total blade failure, as defined in section 6.5.4 should not cause yielding in shafts and shaft components. The loading shall consist of the combined axial, bending, and torsion loads, wherever this is significant. The minimum safety factor against yielding must be 1.0 for bending and torsional stresses.

The loading shall consist of the combined axial, bending, and torsion loads, wherever this is significant. The minimum safety factor against yielding must be 1.0 for bending and torsional stresses.

```turtle
                                    sh:property [
                                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                                        sh:maxCount 1
                                    ] ;
                                    sh:property [
                                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
                                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                                        sh:maxCount 1
                                    ]
                                ]
                            ]
                        ]
                        [
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path nltl:combinedAxialBendingTorsionLoad ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                                sh:node [
                                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                                    sh:property [
                                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                                        sh:maxCount 1
                                    ] ;
                                    sh:property [
                                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
                                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                                        sh:maxCount 1
                                    ]
                                ]
                            ]
                        ]
                    )
                ]
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:shaftLineLoadCaseType ;
                        sh:in (
                            nltl:extremeOperationalLoadCase
                            nltl:fatigueLoadCase
                        ) ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ) ;
            sh:message "The load case shall identify whether it is a blade-failure, extreme-operational, or fatigue load case; blade-failure cases shall provide the declared load inputs."
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For a blade-failure load case, each linked propeller shaft line component shall have exactly one bending and one torsional yield safety factor, each at least 1.0 and expressed as a dimensionless quantity." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasPropulsionLoadCase ?loadCase .
                ?loadCase nltl:shaftLineLoadCaseType nltl:bladeFailureLoadCase .
                $this nltl:hasPropellerShaftLineComponent ?component .
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        ?component nltl:bendingYieldSafetyFactor ?bending .
                        ?bending a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                                 qudt:numericValue ?bendingValue ;
                                 qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                        FILTER (
                            datatype(?bendingValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                            ?bendingValue >= 1
                        )
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?bending qudt:numericValue ?otherBendingValue .
                            FILTER (?otherBendingValue != ?bendingValue)
                        }
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?bending qudt:unit ?otherBendingUnit .
                            FILTER (
                                ?otherBendingUnit !=
                                <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS>
                            )
                        }
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?component nltl:bendingYieldSafetyFactor ?otherBending .
                            FILTER (?otherBending != ?bending)
                        }
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        ?component nltl:torsionalYieldSafetyFactor ?torsional .
                        ?torsional a qudt:QuantityValue ;
                                  qudt:numericValue ?torsionalValue ;
                                  qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> .
                        FILTER (
                            datatype(?torsionalValue) = xsd:decimal &&
                            ?torsionalValue >= 1
                        )
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?torsional qudt:numericValue ?otherTorsionalValue .
                            FILTER (?otherTorsionalValue != ?torsionalValue)
                        }
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?torsional qudt:unit ?otherTorsionalUnit .
                            FILTER (
                                ?otherTorsionalUnit !=
                                <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS>
                            )
                        }
                        FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                            ?component nltl:torsionalYieldSafetyFactor ?otherTorsional .
                            FILTER (?otherTorsional != ?torsional)
                        }
                    }
                )
            }
        """
    ] .

```

```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF112ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPropellerShaftLineComponent ;
        sh:class nltl:propellerShaftLineComponent ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF112ComponentShape ;
        sh:message "The ship shall identify a propeller shaft line component with bending and torsional yield safety factors."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPropulsionLoadCase ;
        sh:class nltl:loadCase ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node gen:TRF112LoadCaseShape ;
        sh:message "The ship shall identify an applicable propulsion load case for the total blade-failure load."
    ] .

gen:TRF112LoadCaseShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:shaftLineLoadCaseType ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:bladeFailureLoadCase ;
        sh:message "The propulsion load case shall be identified as the total blade-failure load case."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bladeFailureUltimateLoad ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The blade-failure load case shall provide the externally calculated ultimate load in kN."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:combinedAxialBendingTorsionLoad ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The blade-failure load case shall provide the externally calculated combined axial, bending, and torsion load in kN."
    ] .

gen:TRF112ComponentShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:bendingYieldSafetyFactor ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:minInclusive 1.0
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/UNITLESS> ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The externally calculated bending yield safety factor shall be at least 1.0."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:torsionalYieldSafetyFactor ;
```

Full unmodified RDF Turtle:
```turtle
@prefix ex: <https://w3id.org/nltl/benchmark/imo-batch-f/IMO-058-P02/> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .

    nltl:hasExposedFireMainSection ex:section1,
```

```turtle
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasExposedFireMainSection ;
        sh:class nltl:exposedFireMainSectionItem ;
        sh:node [
            a sh:NodeShape ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:fireMainSectionIsolationCapability ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "Each exposed fire-main section shall have isolation capability."
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:fireMainSectionDrainageMeansPresent ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue true ;
                sh:message "Each exposed fire-main section shall have means of drainage."
            ]
        ]
    ] .
```

Original source text:
Ships proceeding to latitudes over 80 degrees shall be fitted with at least one GNSS compass or equivalent, which shall be connected to the ship's main and emergency source of power.
Normalized requirement:
IF planned_or_actual_latitude_deg > 80, THEN GNSS_compass_or_equivalent_count >= 1 AND connected_to_main_power = true AND connected_to_emergency_power = true.
Required inputs: Voyage latitude, compass inventory, and power connections

```turtle
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:plannedOrActualLatitude ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DEG
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:plannedOrActualLatitude ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:maxInclusive "80"^^xsd:decimal
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:plannedOrActualLatitude ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:minExclusive "80"^^xsd:decimal
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:gnssCompassOrEquivalentCount ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
                sh:minInclusive 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:connectedToMainPower ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:class nltl:benchmarkEntity
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:connectedToEmergencyPower ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:nodeKind sh:IRI ;
                sh:class nltl:benchmarkEntity
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:message "For ships proceeding over 80 degrees latitude, at least one GNSS compass or equivalent and connections to main and emergency power are required." .

```

```turtle
<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-087-ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:plannedOrActualLatitude ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DEG ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:gnssCompassOrEquivalentCount ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "For a planned or actual latitude over 80 degrees, the ship shall have at least one GNSS compass or equivalent connected to both main and emergency power." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this nltl:plannedOrActualLatitude ?latitudeNode .
    ?latitudeNode qudt:numericValue ?latitude .
    FILTER (?latitude > "80"^^xsd:decimal)
    FILTER (
        NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:gnssCompassOrEquivalentCount ?count .
            FILTER (?count >= 1)
        }
        ||
        NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:connectedToMainPower ?mainPower .
        }
        ||
        NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:connectedToEmergencyPower ?emergencyPower .
        }
    )
}
"""
    ] .
```

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-028-20260915T103522739270Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The Manual shall include risk-based procedures to be followed for: contacting emergency response providers for salvage, search and rescue (SAR), spill response, etc., as applicable; and, in the case of ships ice strengthened in accordance with chapter 3, procedures for maintaining life support and ship integrity in the event of prolonged entrapment by ice.
Normalized requirement:
PWOM shall include applicable emergency-provider contact procedures; IF ship_ice_strengthened = true, THEN PWOM shall also include prolonged-ice-entrapment life-support and integrity procedures.
Required inputs: PWOM, emergency-contact procedure, ice-strengthening status, and entrapment procedure

Original source text:
When the cross-sectional area of the local frame exceeds the cross-sectional area of the attached plate flange, the plastic neutral axis is z_na = (100*A_fn + h_w*t_wn - 1000*t_pn*s)/(2*t_wn) [mm], and the net effective plastic section modulus is Z_p = t_pn*s*(z_na + t_pn/2)*sin(phi_w) + (((h_w - z_na)^2 + z_na^2)*t_wn*sin(phi_w))/2000 + A_fn*((h_fc - z_na)*sin(phi_w) - b_w*cos(phi_w))/10 [cm^3]. For oblique framing (70 deg > Omega > 20 deg), linear interpolation is to be used.

Original source text:
(iii) Within the Bow area of all Polar Class ships, and within the Bow Intermediate Icebelt area of Polar Class PC6 and PC7, the ice load parameters are functions of the actual bow shape. To determine the ice load parameters (Pavg, b and w), it is required to calculate the following ice load characteristics for sub-regions of the bow area; shape coefficient (fai), total glancing impact force (Fi), line load (Qi) and pressure (Pi).
Normalized requirement:
(iii) Within the Bow area of all Polar Class ships, and within the Bow Intermediate Icebelt area of Polar Class PC6 and PC7, the ice load parameters are functions of the actual bow shape. To determine the ice load parameters (Pavg, b and w), it is required to calculate the following ice load characteristics for sub-regions of the bow area; shape coefficient (fai), total glancing impact force (Fi), line load (Qi) and pressure (Pi).

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-127> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:airReceiverServesAdditionalPurpose ;
        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The ship must state whether the air receivers serve an additional purpose."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:airReceiverServesAdditionalPurpose ;
                sh:hasValue false
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:airReceiverServesAdditionalPurpose ;
                sh:hasValue true
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:airReceiverCapacity ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:M3 ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:additionalPurposeRequiredAirCapacity ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:node [
                    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ] ;
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path qudt:unit ;
                        sh:hasValue unit:M3 ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1
                    ]
                ]
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Air receiver capacity must be at least the capacity required for additional purposes." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:airReceiverServesAdditionalPurpose true ;
                      nltl:airReceiverCapacity ?capacity ;
                      nltl:additionalPurposeRequiredAirCapacity ?required .
                ?capacity qudt:numericValue ?capacityValue ;
                          qudt:unit unit:M3 .
                ?required qudt:numericValue ?requiredValue ;
                          qudt:unit unit:M3 .
                FILTER (
                    ?capacityValue < ?requiredValue
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gs:TRF127_AirReceiverAdditionalCapacityShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:airReceiverServesAdditionalPurpose ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:hasValue false ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1 ;
                sh:message "The air receiver additional-purpose applicability must be explicitly recorded as false when the additional-capacity condition does not apply."
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:and (
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:airReceiverServesAdditionalPurpose ;
                        sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                        sh:hasValue true ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:maxCount 1 ;
                        sh:message "The air receiver additional-purpose applicability must be explicitly recorded as true when additional capacity is required."
                    ]
                ]
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:airReceiverCapacity ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minCount 1
                            ] ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                                sh:hasValue unit:M3 ;
                                sh:minCount 1 ;
                                sh:maxCount 1
                            ]
                        ] ;
                        sh:message "An air receiver capacity in cubic metres is required when the receiver serves an additional purpose."
                    ]
                ]
                [
                    sh:property [
                        sh:path nltl:additionalPurposeRequiredAirCapacity ;
                        sh:minCount 1 ;
                        sh:node [
                            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
                            sh:property [
                                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                                sh:minCount 1
                            ]
                        ] ;
                        sh:message "The required additional-purpose air capacity is required when the receiver serves an additional purpose."
                    ]
                ]
                [
                    sh:sparql [
                        sh:message "Air receiver capacity must be at least the capacity required for additional purposes." ;
                        sh:select """
                            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
                            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
                            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
                            SELECT $this
                            WHERE {
                                $this nltl:airReceiverCapacity ?capacity .
                                ?capacity qudt:numericValue ?capacityValue .
                                $this nltl:additionalPurposeRequiredAirCapacity ?required .
                                ?required qudt:numericValue ?requiredValue .
                                FILTER ( ?capacityValue < ?requiredValue )
                            }
                        """
                    ]
                ]
            )
        ]
    ) .

```

REQUIREMENT 020: TRF-040

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 20
Batch: BATCH_02
Requirement ID: TRF-040
Source family: TRAFICOM
Verification mode: DIRECT_STATIC
Source document: Ice Class Regulations and the Application Thereof
Source revision: TRAFICOM/68863/03.04.01.00/2021
Clause / section / page: 4.3.1 / Vertical extension of ice strengthening for plating (ice belt) / 16
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-040-20260915T125202944719Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
In addition, the following areas shall be strengthened: Fore foot: For ice class IA Super, the shell plating below the ice belt from the stem to a position five main frame spacings abaft of the point where the bow profile departs from the keel line shall be ice-strengthened in the same way as the bow region. Upper bow ice belt: For ice classes IA Super and IA on ships with an open water service speed equal to or exceeding 18 knots, the shell plate from the upper limit of the ice belt to 2 m above it and from the stem to a position at least 0.2 L abaft of the forward perpendicular shall be ice-strengthened in the same way as the midbody region. A similar strengthening of the bow region is also advisable for a ship with a lower service speed when, on the basis of the model tests, for example, it is evident that the ship will have a high bow wave. Sidescuttles shall not be situated in the ice belt. If the weather deck on any part of the ship is situated below the upper limit of the ice belt (e.g. in the way of the well of a raised quarter decker), the bulwark shall be provided with at least the same strength as is required for the shell in the ice belt. The strength of the construction of the freeing ports shall meet the same requirements.
Normalized requirement:
IF iceClass = 'IA Super', THEN forefoot_shell_strengthening_status = bow_region_equivalent for the specified five-main-frame-spacing extent. IF iceClass IN {'IA Super','IA'} AND open_water_service_speed_kn >= 18, THEN upper_bow_ice_belt_strengthening_status = midbody_equivalent for the area from the ice-belt upper limit to 2 m above it and at least 0.2*L aft of the forward perpendicular. sidescuttle_in_ice_belt = false. IF weather_deck_below_ice_belt_upper_limit = true, THEN bulwark_strength_status and freeing_port_strength_status shall meet the ice-belt shell requirement. The lower-speed model-test sentence is advisory and is not encoded as a mandatory constraint.
Required inputs: iceClass, spacing, thickness, designOperationSpeedInIce_kn
